The purpose of a tokenizer is to turn text into token identifiers and reconstruct that text from identifiers. A vocabulary should reflect the distributions of the documents the model will actually read. Natural prose, programming syntax, punctuation, mathematical notation, and whitespace all matter for efficiency.

Astronomy. A star emits light because nuclear fusion releases energy in its interior. The pressure of hot gas and radiation balances the inward pull of gravity for much of its lifetime. A stellar spectrum carries clues about temperature, chemical composition, and motion relative to an observer.

Planetary science. The seasons result mainly from the tilt of a planet’s rotation axis, not a simple change in its distance from its star. At high latitudes the length of daylight changes dramatically over the year, while regions near the equator experience less variation.

Meteorology. Warm, moist air can rise, cool, and condense into clouds. Whether precipitation forms depends on humidity, temperature, and atmospheric motion. Forecasts combine observations with numerical models, but small initial errors can grow over time.

Physics. A force can change the velocity of an object. Momentum depends on both mass and velocity, and it is conserved in an isolated system. A free-body diagram clarifies which forces act on an object and prevents confusion between forces and motion.

Thermodynamics. Heat flows spontaneously from a hotter region to a cooler one when they are in thermal contact. Temperature indicates thermal state, whereas heat is energy transferred. Insulation reduces the rate of thermal transfer, but real processes still involve losses and imperfections.

Optics. Light can reflect, refract, diffract, and interfere. A lens changes the direction of rays to form an image under suitable conditions. The wavelength of visible light influences its perceived color, while lenses and sensors determine how an image is recorded.

Materials science. A material’s strength, stiffness, toughness, and hardness describe different properties. The internal structure of metals, polymers, and ceramics helps explain why they perform differently under stress.

Evolution. Evolution refers to changes in heritable characteristics of populations across generations. Natural selection can favor traits associated with successful reproduction in a particular environment. Random mutation and genetic drift also affect populations, so evolutionary change is not necessarily directed toward a single goal.

A clear technical report begins by stating the problem, the available evidence, and the criteria for success. It distinguishes measured results from estimates and gives enough detail that another person can reproduce the work. When something fails, the report should describe the unexpected behavior rather than hide it.

At the community library, volunteers cataloged donated books and repaired worn shelves. A public meeting helped residents decide where to add lighting, seating, and accessible paths. The final plan balanced cost, safety, and the needs of people who use the building every day.

A school science club compared the rates at which different cups cooled after being filled with warm water. The students controlled the starting temperature and measured each cup at regular intervals. Their notes included the room conditions, uncertainty of the thermometer, and possible sources of error.

The hiking trail crossed a dry wash, climbed a rocky ridge, and descended toward an area shaded by cottonwood trees. The group carried water, checked the forecast, and chose a route that fit the time available. A map helped them understand elevation changes that were less obvious on the ground.

An orchestra rehearses not only the correct notes but also balance, phrasing, and timing. Players listen across sections so the melody remains clear while accompanying instruments support it. The conductor uses gestures to communicate entrances, tempo, and expression.

In a debate, two speakers can agree on the facts and still disagree about priorities. A productive response identifies the claim being disputed, provides evidence, and acknowledges competing goals. Respectful criticism addresses an argument rather than the person who made it.

A developer debugging a program first tries to reproduce the failure consistently. They isolate the smallest input that triggers it, inspect logs, and compare actual behavior with the specification. A regression test protects against accidentally reintroducing the same bug.

When designing a machine, an engineer estimates forces before selecting materials and motors. The design is then tested with realistic loads, including edge cases that are unlikely but important. Results may lead to stronger brackets, better bearings, or a revised transmission.

A historian reading a letter pays attention to the date, author, audience, and purpose. The letter may reveal what someone believed at the time, but it cannot answer every question about the wider society. Comparing several sources can expose omissions and contradictions.

A gardener monitors sunlight, moisture, and soil conditions throughout the season. Seeds that germinate well under one set of conditions may struggle in another. Small observations recorded over several weeks help explain changes in growth.

Public transit planners study origin and destination patterns rather than treating every road as independent. A reliable timetable can be as important as raw vehicle speed. Transfers, walking distance, and accessibility influence whether a route is useful.

An editor preparing an article checks terminology, numerical claims, and sources. Short sentences can improve clarity, but complex ideas sometimes require careful qualification. Headings should reflect the content beneath them instead of promising more than the evidence supports.

from collections import defaultdict

def count_groups(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 2] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = count_groups([-16, -5, 6, 17, 28])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def measure_latency(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

import torch
from torch import nn

class MetricRow(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 4):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

export function aggregate_scores(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 6);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

export function estimate_velocity(items: ReadonlyArray<TaskItem>): TaskItem[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 7)

}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn find_neighbors(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(8)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [26, 37, 48, 59, 70];
    println!("{:?}", find_neighbors(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t normalize_values(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 204u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>

std::vector<int> encode_tokens(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

    public static List<Integer> calculate_energy(List<Integer> input) {

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func build_index(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(build_index([]int{61, 72, -14, -3, 8})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn parse_events(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 14) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 68, 79, -7, 4, 15 };
    _ = parse_events(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 378 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 15
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

import numpy as np

def update_cache(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

uint32_t merge_tables(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 813u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

std::vector<int> stream_records(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

constexpr int limit = 8;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class Record {
    public static List<Integer> count_groups(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 9 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

data class VectorPoint(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun measure_latency(records: List<VectorPoint>): List<VectorPoint> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 10 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<VectorPoint> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

val sample = listOf(VectorPoint("robot", 900), VectorPoint("sensor", 10))

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func group_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(group_samples([]int{14, 25, 36, 47, 58})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn prepare_batch(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 12) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 21, 32, 43, 54, 65 };
    _ = prepare_batch(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 987 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 13
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

from collections import defaultdict

def parse_events(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 21] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = parse_events([-13, -2, 9, 20, 31])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def evaluate_model(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[-6, 5, 16, 27, 38], [-6, 5, 16, 27, 38]], dtype=np.float32)

import torch
from torch import nn

class TrainingJob(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 23):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def deduplicate_rows(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

key = jax.random.PRNGKey(1306)

export function process_queue(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 2);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

const records = [{category: 'alpha'}, {category: 'beta'}];

type EventLog = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function collect_samples(items: ReadonlyArray<EventLog>): EventLog[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 3)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn merge_results(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(4)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [29, 40, 51, 62, 73];
    println!("{:?}", merge_results(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t compute_checksum(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 1422u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

public final class VectorPoint {
    public static List<Integer> sort_metrics(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {

        }
        return output;
    }
}

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func validate_result(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(validate_result([]int{64, 75, -11, 0, 11})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn project_vector(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 10) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 71, -15, -4, 7, 18 };
    _ = project_vector(&sample);
}

uint32_t validate_schema(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 2031u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

from collections import defaultdict

def deduplicate_rows(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 15] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = deduplicate_rows([40, 51, 62, 73, -13])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def process_queue(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[47, 58, 69, 80, -6], [47, 58, 69, 80, -6]], dtype=np.float32)

import torch
from torch import nn

class EventLog(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 17):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def merge_results(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

key = jax.random.PRNGKey(3133)

export function compute_checksum(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 19);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

const records = [{category: 'alpha'}, {category: 'beta'}];

type Record = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function save_checkpoint(items: ReadonlyArray<Record>): Record[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 20)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn sort_metrics(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(21)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [-15, -4, 7, 18, 29];
    println!("{:?}", sort_metrics(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t update_cache(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 3249u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

#include <vector>
#include <numeric>

std::vector<int> validate_result(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class TrainingJob {
    public static List<Integer> project_vector(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 24 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

data class TaskItem(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun rotate_heading(records: List<TaskItem>): List<TaskItem> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 2 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<TaskItem> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func select_candidates(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(select_candidates([]int{20, 31, 42, 53, 64})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn rank_documents(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 4) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 27, 38, 49, 60, 71 };
    _ = rank_documents(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 3423 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 5
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

from collections import defaultdict

def find_neighbors(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 13] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = find_neighbors([-7, 4, 15, 26, 37])

import numpy as np

def normalize_values(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

func collect_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

}

    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

data class TokenBuffer(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun merge_tables(records: List<TokenBuffer>): List<TokenBuffer> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 19 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<TokenBuffer> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

val sample = listOf(TokenBuffer("robot", 5163), TokenBuffer("sensor", 19))

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func stream_records(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(stream_records([]int{73, -13, -2, 9, 20})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn count_groups(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 21) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 80, -6, 5, 16, 27 };
    _ = count_groups(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 5250 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 22
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

from collections import defaultdict

def calculate_energy(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 7] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = calculate_energy([46, 57, 68, 79, -7])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def validate_schema(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[53, 64, 75, -11, 0], [53, 64, 75, -11, 0]], dtype=np.float32)

import torch
from torch import nn

class VectorPoint(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 9):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def parse_events(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

key = jax.random.PRNGKey(5569)

export function evaluate_model(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 11);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

const records = [{category: 'alpha'}, {category: 'beta'}];

type TrainingJob = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function filter_records(items: ReadonlyArray<TrainingJob>): TrainingJob[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 12)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn deduplicate_rows(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(13)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [-9, 2, 13, 24, 35];
    println!("{:?}", deduplicate_rows(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t process_queue(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 5685u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>
#include <numeric>

std::vector<int> collect_samples(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class TokenBuffer {
    public static List<Integer> merge_results(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 16 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

func save_checkpoint(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

    uint32_t checksum = 6294u;

    "fmt"
    "sort"

func build_index(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

from collections import defaultdict

def parse_events(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 24] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

def evaluate_model(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

export function process_queue(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 5);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

    let mut counts = HashMap::new();

    }

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t compute_checksum(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 7512u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func validate_result(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(validate_result([]int{79, -7, 4, 15, 26})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn project_vector(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 13) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ -11, 0, 11, 22, 33 };
    _ = project_vector(&sample);
}

from collections import defaultdict

def prepare_batch(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 22] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

func select_candidates(numbers []int) []int {

    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))

        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

from collections import defaultdict

def find_neighbors(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 16] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = find_neighbors([8, 19, 30, 41, 52])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def normalize_values(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

export function validate_schema(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 20);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

export function build_index(items: ReadonlyArray<VectorPoint>): VectorPoint[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 21)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn parse_events(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(22)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t evaluate_model(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 9948u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

public final class TaskItem {
    public static List<Integer> deduplicate_rows(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 2 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func collect_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(collect_samples([]int{-12, -1, 10, 21, 32})) }

    }
    return total;
}

    const sample = [_]i32{ -5, 6, 17, 28, 39 };
    _ = merge_results(&sample);
}

    for _, v := range numbers {

    return result
}

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit

export function evaluate_model(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 14);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

type TrainingJob = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function filter_records(items: ReadonlyArray<TrainingJob>): TrainingJob[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 15)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn deduplicate_rows(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(16)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [6, 17, 28, 39, 50];
    println!("{:?}", deduplicate_rows(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t process_queue(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 1768u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

    return values;

public final class TokenBuffer {
    public static List<Integer> merge_results(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();

            if (value % 19 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }

data class RobotState(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun compute_checksum(records: List<RobotState>): List<RobotState> =

        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<RobotState> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func save_checkpoint(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(save_checkpoint([]int{41, 52, 63, 74, -12})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn sort_metrics(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 22) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 48, 59, 70, -16, -5 };
    _ = sort_metrics(&sample);
}

    GROUP BY account_id, event_type

from collections import defaultdict

def count_groups(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 8] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = count_groups([14, 25, 36, 47, 58])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def measure_latency(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[21, 32, 43, 54, 65], [21, 32, 43, 54, 65]], dtype=np.float32)

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func build_index(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(build_index([]int{-6, 5, 16, 27, 38})) }

    }
    return total;
}

from collections import defaultdict

def prepare_batch(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 2] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = prepare_batch([67, 78, -8, 3, 14])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def aggregate_scores(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[74, -12, -1, 10, 21], [74, -12, -1, 10, 21]], dtype=np.float32)

        super().__init__()

        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def find_neighbors(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

export function normalize_values(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 6);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

type TokenBuffer = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function encode_tokens(items: ReadonlyArray<TokenBuffer>): TokenBuffer[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 7)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn calculate_energy(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(8)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [12, 23, 34, 45, 56];
    println!("{:?}", calculate_energy(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t validate_schema(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 4204u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

std::vector<int> build_index(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

public final class MetricRow {
    public static List<Integer> parse_events(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();

        return output;
    }

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func filter_records(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(filter_records([]int{47, 58, 69, 80, -6})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn deduplicate_rows(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 14) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 54, 65, 76, -10, 1 };
    _ = deduplicate_rows(&sample);
}

    for _, v := range numbers {

    return result
}

  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 23);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

const records = [{category: 'alpha'}, {category: 'beta'}];

type VectorPoint = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function build_index(items: ReadonlyArray<VectorPoint>): VectorPoint[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 24)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn parse_events(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(2)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [65, 76, -10, 1, 12];
    println!("{:?}", parse_events(&numbers));
}

    uint32_t checksum = 6031u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func collect_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(collect_samples([]int{3, 14, 25, 36, 47})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn merge_results(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 8) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 10, 21, 32, 43, 54 };
    _ = merge_results(&sample);
}

from collections import defaultdict

def rank_documents(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 17] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = rank_documents([73, -13, -2, 9, 20])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def merge_tables(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[80, -6, 5, 16, 27], [80, -6, 5, 16, 27]], dtype=np.float32)

    def __init__(self, features: int = 19):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

func encode_tokens(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

    }
    return total;
}

from collections import defaultdict

def count_groups(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 11] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = count_groups([29, 40, 51, 62, 73])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def measure_latency(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[36, 47, 58, 69, 80], [36, 47, 58, 69, 80]], dtype=np.float32)

import torch
from torch import nn

class MetricRow(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 13):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def prepare_batch(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

key = jax.random.PRNGKey(8351)

export function aggregate_scores(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 15);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

const records = [{category: 'alpha'}, {category: 'beta'}];

type TaskItem = { id: string; score: number; active?: boolean };

export function estimate_velocity(items: ReadonlyArray<TaskItem>): TaskItem[] {
  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 16)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn find_neighbors(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(17)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [71, -15, -4, 7, 18];
    println!("{:?}", find_neighbors(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t normalize_values(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 8467u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

#include <vector>
#include <numeric>

std::vector<int> encode_tokens(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

public final class RobotState {
    public static List<Integer> calculate_energy(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 20 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

data class Record(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun validate_schema(records: List<Record>): List<Record> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 21 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<Record> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func build_index(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(build_index([]int{9, 20, 31, 42, 53})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn parse_events(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 23) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 16, 27, 38, 49, 60 };
    _ = parse_events(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 8641 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 24
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

func group_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn calculate_energy(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(11)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [27, 38, 49, 60, 71];
    println!("{:?}", calculate_energy(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t validate_schema(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 287u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>
#include <numeric>

std::vector<int> build_index(std::vector<int> values) {
    std::sort(values.begin(), values.end());
    values.erase(std::unique(values.begin(), values.end()), values.end());
    return values;
}

constexpr int limit = 13;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public final class MetricRow {
    public static List<Integer> parse_events(List<Integer> input) {
        List<Integer> output = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 14 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

data class SensorFrame(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun evaluate_model(records: List<SensorFrame>): List<SensorFrame> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 15 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<SensorFrame> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func filter_records(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(filter_records([]int{62, 73, -13, -2, 9})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn deduplicate_rows(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 17) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 69, 80, -6, 5, 16 };
    _ = deduplicate_rows(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 461 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 18
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

from collections import defaultdict

def project_vector(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 3] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = project_vector([35, 46, 57, 68, 79])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def rotate_heading(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

values = np.array([[42, 53, 64, 75, -11], [42, 53, 64, 75, -11]], dtype=np.float32)

from torch import nn

class BatchData(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, features: int = 5):
        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.activation(self.projection(x)) + x

export function merge_tables(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 7);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

func estimate_velocity(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)

    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

def normalize_values(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

from collections import defaultdict

def rank_documents(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 20] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = rank_documents([-9, 2, 13, 24, 35])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def merge_tables(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

        super().__init__()

        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:

export function measure_latency(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 24);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn prepare_batch(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(3)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

    let numbers = [33, 44, 55, 66, 77];
    println!("{:?}", prepare_batch(&numbers));

#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t aggregate_scores(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 2723u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func encode_tokens(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(encode_tokens([]int{68, 79, -7, 4, 15})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn calculate_energy(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 9) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 75, -11, 0, 11, 22 };
    _ = calculate_energy(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 2897 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 10
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

    return result
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t normalize_values(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 4550u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func build_index(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(build_index([]int{24, 35, 46, 57, 68})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn parse_events(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 3) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 31, 42, 53, 64, 75 };
    _ = parse_events(&sample);
}

from collections import defaultdict

def sort_metrics(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 12] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = sort_metrics([-3, 8, 19, 30, 41])

import numpy as np

def update_cache(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

export function rotate_heading(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 16);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

fn rank_documents(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {

from collections import defaultdict

def project_vector(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 6] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = project_vector([50, 61, 72, -14, -3])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def rotate_heading(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

from torch import nn

        super().__init__()
        self.projection = nn.Linear(features, features)
        self.activation = nn.GELU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:

import jax.numpy as jnp

@jax.jit
def rank_documents(x: jax.Array, y: jax.Array) -> jax.Array:
    return jnp.where(x > 0, jnp.tanh(x + y), jnp.zeros_like(x))

export function merge_tables(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 10);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

  return items.filter((item) => (item.active ?? true) && item.score >= 11)
              .map((item) => ({ ...item, id: item.id.trim() }));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn count_groups(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(12)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

fn main() {
    let numbers = [-5, 6, 17, 28, 39];
    println!("{:?}", count_groups(&numbers));
}

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t measure_latency(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 6986u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

    return values;

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func estimate_velocity(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(estimate_velocity([]int{30, 41, 52, 63, 74})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn find_neighbors(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 18) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ 37, 48, 59, 70, -16 };
    _ = find_neighbors(&sample);
}

    GROUP BY account_id, event_type

from collections import defaultdict

def deduplicate_rows(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 4] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

        for (int value : input) {
            if (value % 9 == 0) output.add(value);
        }
        return output;
    }
}

data class EventLog(val name: String, val score: Int)

fun normalize_values(records: List<EventLog>): List<EventLog> =
    records.filter { it.score >= 10 }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<EventLog> { it.score }.thenBy { it.name })

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func encode_tokens(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(encode_tokens([]int{-14, -3, 8, 19, 30})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn calculate_energy(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 12) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ -7, 4, 15, 26, 37 };
    _ = calculate_energy(&sample);
}

from collections import defaultdict

def merge_results(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 21] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

result = merge_results([56, 67, 78, -8, 3])
print(result)

import numpy as np

def compute_checksum(matrix: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    center = matrix.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    scale = matrix.std(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (matrix - center) / np.maximum(scale, 1e-8)

export function update_cache(records) {
  const result = new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const key = String(record.category ?? 'unknown');
    result.set(key, (result.get(key) ?? 0) + 2);
  }
  return [...result.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

use std::collections::HashMap;

fn project_vector(values: &[i32]) -> HashMap<i32, usize> {
    let mut counts = HashMap::new();
    for &value in values {
        *counts.entry(value.rem_euclid(4)).or_insert(0) += 1;
    }
    counts
}

    let numbers = [1, 12, 23, 34, 45];
    println!("{:?}", project_vector(&numbers));
}

uint32_t rotate_heading(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 9422u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

uint32_t merge_tables(const uint8_t *data, size_t length) {
    uint32_t checksum = 1242u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
        checksum = (checksum * 33u) ^ (uint32_t)data[i];
    }
    return checksum;
}

package main

import (
    "fmt"
    "sort"
)

func group_samples(numbers []int) []int {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)
    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {
        if !seen[v] {
            seen[v] = true
            result = append(result, v)
        }
    }
    sort.Ints(result)
    return result
}

func main() { fmt.Println(group_samples([]int{-8, 3, 14, 25, 36})) }

const std = @import("std");

fn prepare_batch(values: []const i32) i64 {
    var total: i64 = 0;
    for (values) |v| {
        if (@mod(v, 4) == 0) total += v;
    }
    return total;
}

pub fn main() void {
    const sample = [_]i32{ -1, 10, 21, 32, 43 };
    _ = prepare_batch(&sample);
}

WITH ranked AS (
    SELECT account_id, event_type, COUNT(*) AS event_count,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY account_id ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank_num
    FROM telemetry_events
    WHERE duration_ms >= 1416 AND status_code != 404
    GROUP BY account_id, event_type
)
SELECT account_id, event_type, event_count
FROM ranked
WHERE rank_num <= 5
ORDER BY account_id, event_count DESC;

from collections import defaultdict

def parse_events(values: list[int]) -> dict[int, int]:
    """Count occurrences without changing the original sequence."""
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for value in values:
        counts[value % 13] += 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))

    result := make([]int, 0, len(numbers))
    for _, v := range numbers {

}
